Modified Function Code:
from re_arc.dsl import *
def generate_150deff5() -> dict:
    diff_lb = 0
    diff_ub = 1
    print(f"diff_lb: {diff_lb}")
    print(f"diff_ub: {diff_ub}")

    cols = difference(interval(0, 10, 1), (2, 8))
    print(f"cols: {cols}")

    bo = {(0, 0), (0, 1), (1, 0), (1, 1)}
    ro1 = {(0, 0), (0, 1), (0, 2)}
    ro2 = {(0, 0), (1, 0), (2, 0)}
    boforb = set()
    reforb = set()

    h = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (8, 30))
    print(f"h: {h}")
    w = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (8, 30))
    print(f"w: {w}")

    bgc, fgc = sample(cols, 2)
    print(f"bgc: {bgc}, fgc: {fgc}")

    gi = canvas(bgc, (h, w))
    print(f"gi: {gi}")

    noccs = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (2, (h * w) // 10))
    print(f"noccs: {noccs}")

    inds = asindices(gi)
    print(f"inds: {inds}")

    needsbgc = []
    for k in range(noccs):
        obj, col = choice(((bo, 8), (choice((ro1, ro2)), 2)))
        oh, ow = shape(obj)
        print(f"obj: {obj}, col: {col}, oh: {oh}, ow: {ow}")

        cands = sfilter(inds, lambda ij: ij[0] <= h - oh and ij[1] <= w - ow and shift(obj, ij).issubset(inds))
        print(f"cands: {cands}")

        if col == 8:
            cands = sfilter(cands, lambda ij: ij not in boforb)
        else:
            cands = sfilter(cands, lambda ij: ij not in reforb)

        if len(cands) == 0:
            break

        loc = choice(totuple(cands))
        print(f"loc: {loc}")

        if col == 8:
            boforb.add(add(loc, (-2, 0)))
            boforb.add(add(loc, (2, 0)))
            boforb.add(add(loc, (0, 2)))
            boforb.add(add(loc, (0, -2)))
        if col == 2:
            if obj == ro1:
                reforb.add(add(loc, (0, 3)))
                reforb.add(add(loc, (0, -3)))
            else:
                reforb.add(add(loc, (1, 0)))
                reforb.add(add(loc, (-1, 0)))

        plcd = shift(obj, loc)
        gi = fill(gi, fgc, plcd)
        print(f"plcd: {plcd}, updated gi: {gi}")

        inds = inds - plcd
        print(f"updated inds: {inds}")

    return {'input': gi}
